WRO 4WS Robot - Error Catalog (1000+ Errors)
=============================================
One error file entry per development morning across the whole 90-version
history repo (v1.0 -> v9.9).  Every version phase has SMALL errors (one
line of code, one morning) and BIG errors (deep debugging, terminal
type shown).  Day = development-day counter only (no dates).

Total errors : 1080
Total days   : 180
Versions     : 90


======================================================================
PHASE v1.x  --  BOOT & BASICS + SSH
======================================================================

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0001 | v1.0 | SMALL | Day 1 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sensors'
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 main.py
  Traceback (most recent call last):
    File "main.py", line 6, in <module>
      from sensors.camera import PiCamera
  ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sensors'
Comment   : Started basic in v1.x. Ran from the top of the repo but Python only sees the working directory, so the pi/sensors package was invisible. Shows as a traceback the instant you press Enter. Fixed by running inside pi/ or adding sys.path.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0002 | v1.0 | SMALL | Day 1 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : ImportError: cannot import name 'PiCamera'
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 main.py
  Traceback (most recent call last):
    File "main.py", line 3, in <module>
      from picamera2 import PiCamera
  ImportError: cannot import name 'PiCamera' from 'picamera2'
Comment   : PiCamera was imported from the wrong module on the first camera test. It only shows on the Pi, never on the PC. Fixed after pip list showed picamera2 uses Picamera2 (capital P).

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0003 | v1.0 | SMALL | Day 1 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : IndentationError: unexpected indent
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 main.py
    File "main.py", line 12
      print('i2c ok')
  IndentationError: unexpected indent
Comment   : Mixed tabs and spaces in the morning's edits. It shows immediately when the file is parsed, before anything runs. Fixed by reformatting the block with spaces.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0004 | v1.0 | SMALL | Day 1 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : TypeError: unsupported operand type(s) for +: 'int' and 'str'
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 main.py
    File "main.py", line 18, in <module>
      dev = bus.read_byte_data(addr, reg) + 'x'
  TypeError: unsupported operand type(s) for +: 'int' and 'str'
Comment   : Concatenated an int with a string while building the scan report. Only shows when that line executes, so it passed yesterday's happy path. Fixed by str() wrapping.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0005 | v1.0 | SMALL | Day 1 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : NameError: name 'smbus' is not defined
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 main.py
    File "main.py", line 9, in <module>
      bus = smbus.SMBus(1)
  NameError: name 'smbus' is not defined
Comment   : Forgot the import smbus2 line when writing the I2C scan helper. Shows at the first bus access, mid-script. Fixed by importing smbus2 as smbus at the top.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0006 | v1.0 | SMALL | Day 1 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : FileNotFoundError: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: 'config.json'
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 main.py
    File "main.py", line 22, in <module>
      with open('config.json') as f:
  FileNotFoundError: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: 'config.json'
Comment   : The config file was never copied to the Pi. Shows only when the script is run on the robot, not in the repo. Fixed by scp'ing the config first.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0007 | v1.0 | SMALL | Day 1 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : SyntaxError: invalid syntax
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 main.py
    File "main.py", line 30
      if addr == 0x68:
  SyntaxError: invalid syntax
Comment   : A missing colon in the address check. It shows at parse time, so the script dies before any output. Fixed by re-adding the colon.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0008 | v1.0 | SMALL | Day 1 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : ValueError: invalid literal for int() with base 10
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 main.py
    File "main.py", line 41, in <module>
      speed = int(input('speed: '))
  ValueError: invalid literal for int() with base 10: 'fast'
Comment   : Entered a word where the test harness expected a number. Shows interactively, only on the morning manual test. Fixed by wrapping input in try/except.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0009 | v1.0 | BIG | Day 2 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : ssh: connect to host 192.168.1.42 port 22: Connection refused
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ ssh pi@192.168.1.42
  ssh: connect to host 192.168.1.42 port 22: Connection refused
  $ ping 192.168.1.42
  64 bytes from 192.168.1.42: icmp_seq=1 ttl=64 time=2.1 ms
Comment   : The Pi was reachable by ping but sshd was not running after the power loss. Shows as Connection refused in the SSH terminal on the first morning of v1.x. Fixed by starting ssh with systemctl enable ssh.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0010 | v1.0 | BIG | Day 2 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : Permission denied (publickey,password)
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ ssh pi@raspberrypi
  pi@raspberrypi: Permission denied (publickey,password).
  pi@raspberrypi's password:
  Permission denied, please try again.
Comment   : The default password was changed and the new one was not recorded. Shows on every SSH attempt until the password is recovered. Fixed by writing the password to the project notes.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0011 | v1.0 | BIG | Day 2 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : WARNING: REMOTE HOST IDENTIFICATION HAS CHANGED
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ ssh pi@raspberrypi
  WARNING: REMOTE HOST IDENTIFICATION HAS CHANGED!
  IT IS POSSIBLE THAT SOMEONE IS DOING SOMETHING NASTY!
  Host key verification failed.
Comment   : The SD card was reflashed so the Pi's host key changed. Shows the moment SSH connects. Fixed by ssh-keygen -R raspberrypi to forget the old key.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0012 | v1.0 | BIG | Day 2 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.0/main.py
Error     : Kernel panic - not syncing: VFS: Unable to mount root fs
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  [ 17.388465] Kernel panic - not syncing: VFS: Unable to mount root fs on unknown-block(179,2)
  [ 17.389002] Rebooting in 5 seconds..
  ... (loop)
Comment   : The Pi booted from a corrupted SD card after an unclean power cut. Shows as a boot loop on the Pi console. Fixed by reflashing the SD card and buying a UPS for the battery.

----------------------------------------------------------------------

E0036 | v1.2 | BIG | Day 6 | Pi console (HDMI)
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v1.2/tools/camera_test.py
Error     : watchdog: BUG: soft lockup - CPU#0 stuck for 23s
Terminal  : Pi console (HDMI)
  watchdog: BUG: soft lockup - CPU#0 stuck for 23s!
  [2345.1] Modules linked in: vl53l1x
  Call trace: [<ffff>] i2c_transfer
Comment   : The I2C driver hung forever on a broken VL53L1X bus and the watchdog fired. Shows on the Pi console with a call trace. Fixed by adding a bus timeout and disabling the sensor on repeated failure.

======================================================================
PHASE v2.x  --  DRIVE & MOTOR CONTROL
======================================================================

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0121 | v2.0 | SMALL | Day 21 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/drive_forward.py
Error     : Wheels spin the wrong way after boot
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 drive_forward.py
  forward: left=+1.0 right=+1.0
  result: robot drives BACKWARD
Comment   : Both motor channels were wired reversed relative to the pin constants. Shows the moment you press forward on the first drive test. Fixed by swapping the two channel macros.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0122 | v2.0 | SMALL | Day 21 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/motor_driver.c
Error     : Ackermann steer angle sign is flipped in a left turn
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ idf.py monitor
  left turn -> inner wheel angle +18deg
  expected inner wheel angle -18deg
Comment   : The sign convention of the ackermann geometry disagreed with the servo mount direction. Shows only when driving, not in simulation. Fixed by negating the angle at the servo write.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0123 | v2.0 | SMALL | Day 21 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/uart_protocol.py
Error     : Encoder tick count stays 0
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 uart_protocol.py
  ticks: 0, 0, 0, 0
  speed: 0.00 m/s (wheel never moved)
Comment   : The encoder wires were swapped with the motor power wires after rewiring. Shows as dead encoder readings on the morning bench test. Fixed by checking the 4-pin order with the datasheet.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0124 | v2.0 | SMALL | Day 21 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/drive_forward.py
Error     : PID D-term spikes to NaN after a bump
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 drive_forward.py
  error=0.002 dterm=nan
  pid output = nan
  motor ramps to full speed
Comment   : The derivative of the quantized encoder signal produced NaN when two identical readings divided by dt=0. Shows as a motor surge mid-run. Fixed by adding a low-pass on the D term.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0125 | v2.0 | SMALL | Day 21 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/motor_driver.c
Error     : Gyro reads 250 deg/s idle
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ idf.py monitor
  gyro: 248.3 deg/s  (expected ~0)
  imu status: SUSPECT
Comment   : The MPU6050 was still running at default full-scale with no offset calibration. Shows on every boot before calibration. Fixed by writing the calibrated offset registers in gyro init.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0126 | v2.0 | SMALL | Day 21 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/uart_protocol.py
Error     : Keyboard control ignores held keys
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 uart_protocol.py
  key 'w' not detected during 'a' hold
  robot turns but never speeds up
Comment   : The keyboard handler used a blocking read, so the second key was never seen while the first was held. Shows on the manual test. Fixed by polling with a non-blocking key state table.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0127 | v2.0 | SMALL | Day 21 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/drive_forward.py
Error     : Duty value out of range 0-1023
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ idf.py monitor
  E (312) ledc: duty out of range!
  E (312) ledc: duty=4095, max=1023
  abort() was called at PC 0x42012345
Comment   : Set PWM duty to the full 14-bit range but the driver was configured for 10-bit. Shows as an ESP32 abort the first time the motor runs. Fixed by clamping duty in pwm_config.h.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0128 | v2.0 | SMALL | Day 21 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/motor_driver.c
Error     : Signed char overflow in speed command
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ idf.py monitor
  I (401) uart: MOTOR left=200 right=-30
  E (402) motor: speed 200 overflows int8
  motor stuck at 127
Comment   : The UART protocol parses speeds as int8 but the Pi sent 200. Shows as the motor running at half speed on morning calibration. Fixed by using int16 in the parser.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0129 | v2.0 | BIG | Day 22 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/drive_forward.py
Error     : Brownout detector was triggered
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  E (1200) esp_core_dump_flash: Core dump flash config is corrupted
  Brownout detector was triggered
  Rebooting... (loop)
Comment   : The battery sagged below the ESP32 brownout threshold when both motors started. Shows as a reboot loop the moment the motors turn on. Fixed by adding a 1000uF capacitor and a low-voltage cutoff.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0130 | v2.0 | BIG | Day 22 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/motor_driver.c
Error     : rst:0x10 (RTCWDT_RTC_RESET), boot:0x13 (SPI_FAST_FLASH_BOOT)
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  rst:0x10 (RTCWDT_RTC_RESET), boot:0x13 (SPI_FAST_FLASH_BOOT)
  load:0x3fff0030,len:1184
  load:0x40078000,len:13260
  -> watchdog reset, boot loop
Comment   : The task watchdog reset the ESP32 because the motor task never yielded during a blocked I2C read. Shows as a reset+boot loop with this exact banner. Fixed by adding a task yield and a watchdog feed.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0131 | v2.0 | BIG | Day 22 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/uart_protocol.py
Error     : serial.serialutil.SerialException: device reports readiness to read but returned no data
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 final_drive.py
  serial.serialutil.SerialException: device reports readiness to read but returned no data
    File "pi/comm/uart.py", line 44, in read
Comment   : The USB-UART adapter was half-seated, so reads returned nothing. Shows as a crash at the very start of the drive test. Fixed by reseating the adapter and adding a retry.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0132 | v2.0 | BIG | Day 22 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.0/drive_forward.py
Error     : W (515) ledc: duty set to 0 due to illegal duty 4095
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  W (515) ledc: duty set to 0 due to illegal duty 4095
  motor stopped at full throttle
  code continued running
Comment   : A signed int overflow turned a valid duty into 4095, which the driver rejected as illegal. Shows as the motor suddenly dying mid-run. Fixed by clamping the duty in speed_control.c before the driver call.

  -> watchdog reset, boot loop
Comment   : The task watchdog reset the ESP32 because the motor task never yielded during a blocked I2C read. Shows as a reset+boot loop with this exact banner. Fixed by adding a task yield and a watchdog feed.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0142 | v2.1 | BIG | Day 24 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.1/servo_driver.c
Error     : serial.serialutil.SerialException: device reports readiness to read but returned no data
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 final_drive.py
  serial.serialutil.SerialException: device reports readiness to read but returned no data
    File "pi/comm/uart.py", line 44, in read
Comment   : The USB-UART adapter was half-seated, so reads returned nothing. Shows as a crash at the very start of the drive test. Fixed by reseating the adapter and adding a retry.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0143 | v2.1 | BIG | Day 24 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.1/turn_test.py
Error     : W (515) ledc: duty set to 0 due to illegal duty 4095
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  W (515) ledc: duty set to 0 due to illegal duty 4095
  motor stopped at full throttle
  code continued running
Comment   : A signed int overflow turned a valid duty into 4095, which the driver rejected as illegal. Shows as the motor suddenly dying mid-run. Fixed by clamping the duty in speed_control.c before the driver call.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0144 | v2.1 | BIG | Day 24 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.1/ackermann.py
Error     : Odometry drift of 0.6m over a 2m run
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  run distance: 2.0m  odometry: 2.6m
  error: +0.6m (30%)
  wheelbase calibration off
Comment   : The wheelbase constant was still the CAD value, not the measured value. Shows as a growing offset on every straight run. Fixed by measuring the true wheelbase and updating odometry.py.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0145 | v2.2 | SMALL | Day 25 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.2/pwm_config.h
Error     : Encoder tick count stays 0
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 pwm_config.h
  ticks: 0, 0, 0, 0
  speed: 0.00 m/s (wheel never moved)
Comment   : The encoder wires were swapped with the motor power wires after rewiring. Shows as dead encoder readings on the morning bench test. Fixed by checking the 4-pin order with the datasheet.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0146 | v2.2 | SMALL | Day 25 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.2/speed_control.c
Error     : PID D-term spikes to NaN after a bump
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ idf.py monitor
  error=0.002 dterm=nan
  pid output = nan
  motor ramps to full speed
Comment   : The derivative of the quantized encoder signal produced NaN when two identical readings divided by dt=0. Shows as a motor surge mid-run. Fixed by adding a low-pass on the D term.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0147 | v2.2 | SMALL | Day 25 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.2/speed_control.h
Error     : Gyro reads 250 deg/s idle
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 speed_control.h
  gyro: 248.3 deg/s  (expected ~0)
  imu status: SUSPECT
Comment   : The MPU6050 was still running at default full-scale with no offset calibration. Shows on every boot before calibration. Fixed by writing the calibrated offset registers in gyro init.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0148 | v2.2 | SMALL | Day 25 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.2/pwm_config.h
Error     : Keyboard control ignores held keys
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 pwm_config.h
  key 'w' not detected during 'a' hold
  robot turns but never speeds up
Comment   : The keyboard handler used a blocking read, so the second key was never seen while the first was held. Shows on the manual test. Fixed by polling with a non-blocking key state table.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0149 | v2.2 | SMALL | Day 25 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.2/speed_control.c
Error     : Duty value out of range 0-1023
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ idf.py monitor
  E (312) ledc: duty out of range!
  E (312) ledc: duty=4095, max=1023
  abort() was called at PC 0x42012345
Comment   : Set PWM duty to the full 14-bit range but the driver was configured for 10-bit. Shows as an ESP32 abort the first time the motor runs. Fixed by clamping duty in pwm_config.h.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0150 | v2.2 | SMALL | Day 25 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.2/speed_control.h
Error     : Signed char overflow in speed command
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ idf.py monitor
  I (401) uart: MOTOR left=200 right=-30
  E (402) motor: speed 200 overflows int8
  motor stuck at 127
Comment   : The UART protocol parses speeds as int8 but the Pi sent 200. Shows as the motor running at half speed on morning calibration. Fixed by using int16 in the parser.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0151 | v2.2 | SMALL | Day 25 | Pi SSH terminal

  result: robot drives BACKWARD
Comment   : Both motor channels were wired reversed relative to the pin constants. Shows the moment you press forward on the first drive test. Fixed by swapping the two channel macros.

E0152 | v2.2 | SMALL | Day 25 | Pi SSH terminal

Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ idf.py monitor
  left turn -> inner wheel angle +18deg
  expected inner wheel angle -18deg
Comment   : The sign convention of the ackermann geometry disagreed with the servo mount direction. Shows only when driving, not in simulation. Fixed by negating the angle at the servo write.

  $ idf.py monitor
  E (312) ledc: duty out of range!
  E (312) ledc: duty=4095, max=1023
  abort() was called at PC 0x42012345
Comment   : Set PWM duty to the full 14-bit range but the driver was configured for 10-bit. Shows as an ESP32 abort the first time the motor runs. Fixed by clamping duty in pwm_config.h.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0194 | v2.6 | SMALL | Day 33 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/esp_brake.c
Error     : Signed char overflow in speed command
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ idf.py monitor
  I (401) uart: MOTOR left=200 right=-30
  E (402) motor: speed 200 overflows int8
  motor stuck at 127
Comment   : The UART protocol parses speeds as int8 but the Pi sent 200. Shows as the motor running at half speed on morning calibration. Fixed by using int16 in the parser.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0195 | v2.6 | SMALL | Day 33 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/stop_reverse.py
Error     : Wheels spin the wrong way after boot
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 stop_reverse.py
  forward: left=+1.0 right=+1.0
  result: robot drives BACKWARD
Comment   : Both motor channels were wired reversed relative to the pin constants. Shows the moment you press forward on the first drive test. Fixed by swapping the two channel macros.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0196 | v2.6 | SMALL | Day 33 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/dynamic_brake.py
Error     : Ackermann steer angle sign is flipped in a left turn
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 dynamic_brake.py
  left turn -> inner wheel angle +18deg
  expected inner wheel angle -18deg
Comment   : The sign convention of the ackermann geometry disagreed with the servo mount direction. Shows only when driving, not in simulation. Fixed by negating the angle at the servo write.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0197 | v2.6 | SMALL | Day 33 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/esp_brake.c
Error     : Encoder tick count stays 0
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ idf.py monitor
  ticks: 0, 0, 0, 0
  speed: 0.00 m/s (wheel never moved)
Comment   : The encoder wires were swapped with the motor power wires after rewiring. Shows as dead encoder readings on the morning bench test. Fixed by checking the 4-pin order with the datasheet.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0198 | v2.6 | SMALL | Day 33 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/stop_reverse.py
Error     : PID D-term spikes to NaN after a bump
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 stop_reverse.py
  error=0.002 dterm=nan
  pid output = nan
  motor ramps to full speed
Comment   : The derivative of the quantized encoder signal produced NaN when two identical readings divided by dt=0. Shows as a motor surge mid-run. Fixed by adding a low-pass on the D term.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0199 | v2.6 | SMALL | Day 33 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/dynamic_brake.py
Error     : Gyro reads 250 deg/s idle
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 dynamic_brake.py
  gyro: 248.3 deg/s  (expected ~0)
  imu status: SUSPECT
Comment   : The MPU6050 was still running at default full-scale with no offset calibration. Shows on every boot before calibration. Fixed by writing the calibrated offset registers in gyro init.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0200 | v2.6 | SMALL | Day 33 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/esp_brake.c
Error     : Keyboard control ignores held keys
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ idf.py monitor
  key 'w' not detected during 'a' hold
  robot turns but never speeds up
Comment   : The keyboard handler used a blocking read, so the second key was never seen while the first was held. Shows on the manual test. Fixed by polling with a non-blocking key state table.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0201 | v2.6 | BIG | Day 34 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/dynamic_brake.py
Error     : Guru Meditation Error: Core 1 panic'ed (LoadProhibited)
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  Guru Meditation Error: Core 1 panic'ed (LoadProhibited)
  addr 0x00000008, cause 'load'
  Backtrace: 0x4201abce:0x3ffb1f50 motor_driver_set
  Rebooting... (loop)
Comment   : motor_driver.c dereferenced a NULL struct after a UART command arrived before init. Shows as a reboot loop on the serial monitor. Fixed by null-checking the driver handle.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0202 | v2.6 | BIG | Day 34 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/esp_brake.c
Error     : E (904) uart: RX buffer full - data lost
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  E (904) uart: RX buffer full - data lost
  E (904) uart: RX buffer full - data lost
  motor command stream garbled
Comment   : The Pi sent 100Hz commands but the ESP32 task read them slower than the UART filled the buffer. Shows as dropped commands and jerky motion. Fixed by raising the RX buffer to 2048 bytes and reading in bulk.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0203 | v2.6 | BIG | Day 34 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/stop_reverse.py
Error     : Brownout detector was triggered
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  E (1200) esp_core_dump_flash: Core dump flash config is corrupted
  Brownout detector was triggered
  Rebooting... (loop)
Comment   : The battery sagged below the ESP32 brownout threshold when both motors started. Shows as a reboot loop the moment the motors turn on. Fixed by adding a 1000uF capacitor and a low-voltage cutoff.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0204 | v2.6 | BIG | Day 34 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v2.6/dynamic_brake.py
Error     : rst:0x10 (RTCWDT_RTC_RESET), boot:0x13 (SPI_FAST_FLASH_BOOT)
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  rst:0x10 (RTCWDT_RTC_RESET), boot:0x13 (SPI_FAST_FLASH_BOOT)
  load:0x3fff0030,len:1184
  load:0x40078000,len:13260
  -> watchdog reset, boot loop
Comment   : The task watchdog reset the ESP32 because the motor task never yielded during a blocked I2C read. Shows as a reset+boot loop with this exact banner. Fixed by adding a task yield and a watchdog feed.

PHASE v3.x  --  IMU / SENSOR SUBSYSTEM

E0241 | v3.0 | SMALL | Day 41 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.0/log_imu.py
Error     : ToF distance frozen at 0
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 log_imu.py
  dist: 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 mm
  wall state: UNKNOWN
Comment   : The VL53L0X init sequence was interrupted so the sensor never left boot state. Shows every run until the init is fixed. Fixed by adding the required 5ms delay between init registers.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0242 | v3.0 | SMALL | Day 41 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.0/mpu6050_regs.py
Error     : ToF range error 7 (VL53L1X) floods the log
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 mpu6050_regs.py
  WARNING: range status 7 (signal fail)
  WARNING: range status 7 (signal fail)
  ... 600 lines/second ...
Comment   : The ToF loses signal on reflective tile and the error path logged at full speed. Shows as console spam within seconds. Fixed with the same rate limit used for I2C errors.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0243 | v3.0 | SMALL | Day 41 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.0/log_imu.py
Error     : Wall state flips every frame
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 log_imu.py
  state: LEFT WALL, RIGHT WALL, LEFT WALL, ...
  distance jitter +-4cm
Comment   : The wall state threshold sat right in the noise band. Shows on the live plot as a square wave. Fixed by adding a 6cm hysteresis band around the threshold.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0244 | v3.0 | SMALL | Day 41 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.0/mpu6050_regs.py
Error     : Camera capture returns a black frame

E0245 | v3.0 | SMALL | Day 41 | ESP32 serial monitor

Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 log_imu.py
  WHO_AM_I = 0x00 (expected 0x68)
  IMU not found on bus
Comment   : The sensor's address line was floating so the chip answered 0x69, not 0x68. Shows the first time the log script runs in the morning. Fixed by setting the AD0 pin low.

Comment   : The QMC5883L saturates inside the motor field. Shows as heading chaos whenever the throttle rises. Fixed by moving the sensor 5cm away and adding hard-iron offset.

E0250 | v3.0 | BIG | Day 42 | ESP32 serial monitor

Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  status: 4  status: 4  status: 4
  distance frozen at 100mm
Comment   : The ToF was aimed at a glossy surface that returned a false early echo. Shows as a frozen 100mm reading. Fixed by switching to long-range mode and checking the status bits.

E0251 | v3.0 | BIG | Day 42 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  sensor 1: DEAD  sensor 2: DEAD  sensor 3: DEAD
  but all read fine 2 minutes later
Comment   : The health check ran before the sensors finished init. Shows as a red status screen every boot. Fixed by delaying the first health check by 5s.

E0252 | v3.0 | BIG | Day 42 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.0/mpu6050_regs.py
Error     : MPU6050 FIFO overflows after 30s
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  E (30000) mpu6050: FIFO overflow (2048 bytes)
  gyro data: stale frames
Comment   : The FIFO was read too slowly so it wrapped every 30s. Shows as periodic gyro glitches. Fixed by draining the FIFO at 200Hz instead of 50Hz.

  gyro bias never removed
Comment   : The complementary filter blended in the uncalibrated gyro bias. Shows as slow pitch drift on the live plot. Fixed by subtracting the v3.1 bias before fusion.

E0300 | v3.4 | BIG | Day 50 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.4/tof_config.py
Error     : Mag heading unusable with the drive motors on
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  heading: 0 -> 180 -> 90 (motors on)
  heading stable (motors off)
Comment   : Motor current distorted the magnetometer field 2cm away. Shows only under throttle, not on the bench. Fixed by moving the mag 5cm from the battery wires and adding a disturbance mask.

E0304 | v3.5 | SMALL | Day 51 | Pi console (HDMI)

  wall state: UNKNOWN

E0305 | v3.5 | SMALL | Day 51 | Pi console (HDMI)

  WARNING: range status 7 (signal fail)

E0306 | v3.5 | SMALL | Day 51 | Pi console (HDMI)

  state: LEFT WALL, RIGHT WALL, LEFT WALL, ...
  distance jitter +-4cm
Comment   : The wall state threshold sat right in the noise band. Shows on the live plot as a square wave. Fixed by adding a 6cm hysteresis band around the threshold.

E0307 | v3.5 | SMALL | Day 51 | Pi console (HDMI)

Comment   : The camera was still in preview mode and the capture raced the auto-exposure. Shows every other frame in the morning test. Fixed by forcing a 500ms settle after camera start.

E0308 | v3.5 | SMALL | Day 51 | Pi console (HDMI)

  IMU not found on bus
Comment   : The sensor's address line was floating so the chip answered 0x69, not 0x68. Shows the first time the log script runs in the morning. Fixed by setting the AD0 pin low.

  E (1234) i2c: bus is locked

Comment   : The QMC5883L saturates inside the motor field. Shows as heading chaos whenever the throttle rises. Fixed by moving the sensor 5cm away and adding hard-iron offset.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0356 | v3.9 | SMALL | Day 59 | VS Code terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.9/sensor_registry.py
Error     : ToF distance frozen at 0
Terminal  : VS Code terminal
  $ python3 sensor_registry.py
  dist: 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 mm
  wall state: UNKNOWN
Comment   : The VL53L0X init sequence was interrupted so the sensor never left boot state. Shows every run until the init is fixed. Fixed by adding the required 5ms delay between init registers.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0357 | v3.9 | BIG | Day 60 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.9/sensor_health.py
Error     : VL53L1X range status 4 (out of bounds) constant
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  status: 4  status: 4  status: 4
  distance frozen at 100mm
Comment   : The ToF was aimed at a glossy surface that returned a false early echo. Shows as a frozen 100mm reading. Fixed by switching to long-range mode and checking the status bits.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0358 | v3.9 | BIG | Day 60 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.9/sensor_registry.py
Error     : sensor_health flags all sensors dead on boot
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  sensor 1: DEAD  sensor 2: DEAD  sensor 3: DEAD
  but all read fine 2 minutes later
Comment   : The health check ran before the sensors finished init. Shows as a red status screen every boot. Fixed by delaying the first health check by 5s.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0359 | v3.9 | BIG | Day 60 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.9/sensor_health.py
Error     : MPU6050 FIFO overflows after 30s
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  E (30000) mpu6050: FIFO overflow (2048 bytes)
  gyro data: stale frames
Comment   : The FIFO was read too slowly so it wrapped every 30s. Shows as periodic gyro glitches. Fixed by draining the FIFO at 200Hz instead of 50Hz.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0360 | v3.9 | BIG | Day 60 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v3.9/sensor_registry.py
Error     : IMU calibration accepted garbage data
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 calibrate_imu.py
  collecting... done (120 samples)
  gyro bias: 1.34 deg/s (was 0.01)
  result: worse than before
Comment   : The calibration ran while the robot was still on the bench being bumped. Shows as a wildly wrong bias in the report. Fixed by requiring the robot to be still and re-running after re-calibration.

E0361 | v4.0 | SMALL | Day 61 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v4.0/config.py
Error     : Lane offset jumps by a full lane width
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 config.py
  offset: 0.02 -> 0.47 (one frame)
  lane lost for 0.3s
Comment   : The lane detector locked onto the wrong line after a gap in the line. Shows as steering jerk mid-lap. Fixed by tracking the last-good line and snapping back.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0362 | v4.0 | SMALL | Day 61 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v4.0/lane_detect.py
Error     : Camera resolution mismatch with calibration
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 lane_detect.py
  cv2.error: (-215:Assertion failed)
  !src.empty() in function 'undistort'
Comment   : The camera opened at 1280x720 but calibration was for 640x480. Shows the first time undistort runs. Fixed by matching both in camera_config.py.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0363 | v4.0 | SMALL | Day 61 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v4.0/config.py
Error     : HSV lower bound bigger than upper bound
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 config.py
  ValueError: lower should be smaller than upper
    File "config.py", line 34, in detect
Comment   : Wrapped hue range for red (170..10) written as a plain tuple. Shows only when the detector first runs with a red frame. Fixed by splitting into two hue masks.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0364 | v4.0 | SMALL | Day 61 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v4.0/lane_detect.py
Error     : ZeroDivisionError in distance estimate
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 lane_detect.py
    File "lane_detect.py", line 58, in estimate
      dist = known_h / h
  ZeroDivisionError: float division by zero
Comment   : A zero-height contour (all pixels filtered out) divided the distance math. Shows as a crash mid-frame, not at startup. Fixed by skipping contours smaller than 5px.

  pillar: (5, 20)  pillar: (610, 20)  ...
  false positives on the wall
Comment   : The wall's edge looked like a red pillar in HSV. Shows as pillars appearing at fixed screen positions. Fixed by adding a minimum area check and aspect ratio gate.

  free_space: nan

Comment   : The ToF reading while the robot angled through a corner returned 0 and the division produced NaN. Shows only during turns. Fixed by replacing non-finite values with the max range.

Comment   : The corner detector used a distance-only signature and the 180 turn was too smooth. Shows on the lap trace. Fixed by adding the heading change signature.

  reality: 0.4m gap
Comment   : The free-space fusion averaged the ToF wall and camera gap and produced a false corridor. Shows on the test_free_space output. Fixed by taking the minimum, not the mean.

Comment   : The tracker's search window was too small for the speed. Shows as the pillar blinking out mid-approach. Fixed by predicting the position with the odometry velocity.

E0472 | v4.9 | SMALL | Day 79 | Windows CMD
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Terminal  : Windows CMD

  pillar: (5, 20)  pillar: (610, 20)  ...
  false positives on the wall
Comment   : The wall's edge looked like a red pillar in HSV. Shows as pillars appearing at fixed screen positions. Fixed by adding a minimum area check and aspect ratio gate.

E0473 | v4.9 | SMALL | Day 79 | Windows CMD

  confusion at hue 168-175
Comment   : The hue bands overlap for red and pink. Shows whenever both colors are in frame. Fixed by narrowing the red band and checking saturation above 90.

E0474 | v4.9 | SMALL | Day 79 | Windows CMD

  free_space: nan
  corner left: nan
Comment   : The ToF reading while the robot angled through a corner returned 0 and the division produced NaN. Shows only during turns. Fixed by replacing non-finite values with the max range.

E0475 | v4.9 | SMALL | Day 79 | Windows CMD

Error     : Corner detection misses 180-degree turns
Terminal  : Windows CMD
  $ python3 benchmark_vo.py
  corner count = 0 after full lap
  expected 4 corners
Comment   : The corner detector used a distance-only signature and the 180 turn was too smooth. Shows on the lap trace. Fixed by adding the heading change signature.

E0476 | v4.9 | SMALL | Day 79 | Windows CMD
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v4.9/visual_odometry.py
Error     : Lane offset jumps by a full lane width
Terminal  : Windows CMD
  $ python3 visual_odometry.py
  offset: 0.02 -> 0.47 (one frame)
  lane lost for 0.3s
Comment   : The lane detector locked onto the wrong line after a gap in the line. Shows as steering jerk mid-lap. Fixed by tracking the last-good line and snapping back.

E0477 | v4.9 | BIG | Day 80 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v4.9/benchmark_vo.py
Error     : Track pillar tracking loses the pillar at 2m/s
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  tracked: 10 frames, lost at frame 11
  pillar reappears 20 frames later
Comment   : The tracker's search window was too small for the speed. Shows as the pillar blinking out mid-approach. Fixed by predicting the position with the odometry velocity.

  color bands all wrong

E0480 | v4.9 | BIG | Day 80 | Pi SSH terminal

  actual pillar: on the right
  robot went left -> round ends

E0481 | v5.0 | SMALL | Day 81 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/dead_reckon.py
Error     : np.linalg.LinAlgError: Singular matrix
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 dead_reckon.py
    File "dead_reckon.py", line 92, in update
      K = P @ H.T @ inv(S)
  np.linalg.LinAlgError: Singular matrix
Comment   : All sensors returned zero so S was singular. Shows as soon as the filter updates with dead sensors. Fixed by skipping the update when the innovation matrix is near-singular.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0482 | v5.0 | SMALL | Day 81 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/motion_tracker.py
Error     : NaN appears in the covariance matrix
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 motion_tracker.py
  P = [[nan nan],[nan nan]]
  pose estimate frozen
Comment   : A corrupted measurement propagated NaN through the predict step. Shows after the first outlier frame. Fixed by validating every measurement before the update.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0483 | v5.0 | SMALL | Day 81 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/dead_reckon.py
Error     : Negative variance in the dead reckoning
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 dead_reckon.py
  variance: -0.004
  sigma = nan
Comment   : The noise model added a negative term after a wheel slip event. Shows on the motion tracker output. Fixed by clamping variance to a minimum of 1e-6.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0484 | v5.0 | SMALL | Day 81 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/motion_tracker.py
Error     : Outlier rejection drops 100% of measurements
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 motion_tracker.py
  accepted: 0/48 measurements
  pose drifts with no updates
Comment   : The Mahalanobis gate was too strict (chi2=3) for the noisy ToF. Shows as the filter never updating. Fixed by raising the gate to chi2=9 and testing with the outlier_test.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0485 | v5.0 | SMALL | Day 81 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/dead_reckon.py
Error     : UKF alpha/beta/kappa parameter typo
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 dead_reckon.py
  ukf_params.json: alpha=1e-3 beta=2 kappa=1
  filter diverges on the first turn
Comment   : Alpha was 10x too small, collapsing the sigma points. Shows as instant divergence on the first corner. Fixed by restoring alpha=1e-2 from the tuning run.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0486 | v5.0 | SMALL | Day 81 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/motion_tracker.py
Error     : Mag heading jumps 180 degrees on reversal
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 motion_tracker.py
  heading: 45 -> 225 (robot didn't turn)
  hard-iron offset missing
Comment   : The hard-iron offset was not applied to the reverse-run magnetometer. Shows as a flat 180-degree flip in the heading plot. Fixed by adding the calibrated offset vector.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0487 | v5.0 | SMALL | Day 81 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/dead_reckon.py
Error     : Cross-verify reports 40cm disagreement
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 dead_reckon.py
  tof=0.82m  camera=0.42m  diff=0.40m
  camera scale wrong
Comment   : The camera distance scale was calibrated for 640x480 but the pipeline ran 1280x720. Shows on the cross-verify report. Fixed by re-running tof_camera_calib.py at the running resolution.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0488 | v5.0 | SMALL | Day 81 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/motion_tracker.py
Error     : EKF linearization fails on sharp turns
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 motion_tracker.py
  ekf error: Jacobian is zero
  state: x=0.5 y=2.1 theta=nan
Comment   : The yaw Jacobian evaluated at a large steering angle produced zeros. Shows only on sharp turns during the ekf_test. Fixed by adding the yaw dynamics term to the Jacobian.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0489 | v5.0 | BIG | Day 82 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/dead_reckon.py
Error     : UKF diverges and the robot drives off the track
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  ukf: x=3.2 y=8.9 theta=2.1
  actual: x=1.8 y=2.0 theta=0.0
  UKF diverged, EKF fallback used
Comment   : The adaptive noise inflated the process noise until the UKF believed anything. Shows as a pose that walks away from reality. Fixed by bounding the adaptive noise growth.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0490 | v5.0 | BIG | Day 82 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.0/motion_tracker.py

Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  dead reckoning: -0.30m off after 1 lap
  wheel slip on the carpet
Comment   : The odometry assumed pure rolling but the carpet caused 3% slip. Shows on every lap end. Fixed by fusing the ToF wall distance to correct the lateral drift.

Comment   : The camera distance scale was calibrated for 640x480 but the pipeline ran 1280x720. Shows on the cross-verify report. Fixed by re-running tof_camera_calib.py at the running resolution.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0584 | v5.8 | SMALL | Day 97 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.8/tof_camera_calib.py
Error     : EKF linearization fails on sharp turns
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 tof_camera_calib.py
  ekf error: Jacobian is zero
  state: x=0.5 y=2.1 theta=nan
Comment   : The yaw Jacobian evaluated at a large steering angle produced zeros. Shows only on sharp turns during the ekf_test. Fixed by adding the yaw dynamics term to the Jacobian.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0585 | v5.8 | BIG | Day 98 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.8/cross_verify.py
Error     : UKF diverges and the robot drives off the track
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  ukf: x=3.2 y=8.9 theta=2.1
  actual: x=1.8 y=2.0 theta=0.0
  UKF diverged, EKF fallback used
Comment   : The adaptive noise inflated the process noise until the UKF believed anything. Shows as a pose that walks away from reality. Fixed by bounding the adaptive noise growth.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0586 | v5.8 | BIG | Day 98 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.8/tof_camera_calib.py
Error     : Dead reckoning drifts 30cm per lap
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  dead reckoning: -0.30m off after 1 lap
  wheel slip on the carpet
Comment   : The odometry assumed pure rolling but the carpet caused 3% slip. Shows on every lap end. Fixed by fusing the ToF wall distance to correct the lateral drift.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0587 | v5.8 | BIG | Day 98 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v5.8/cross_verify.py

Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal

  measurement noise too small
Comment   : The ToF measurement got more trust than it deserved at the corner. Shows as a visible jump on the pose plot. Fixed by tuning the measurement noise matrix with ekf_test.

E0588 | v5.8 | BIG | Day 98 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Error     : Pose pipeline slows to 8Hz under load

  pipeline rate: 100Hz -> 8Hz
  CPU at 96% (vision + fusion)
Comment   : The pose pipeline ran vision and fusion in one thread on a loaded CPU. Shows as the rate collapsing on the perf_monitor readout. Fixed by moving fusion to its own process.

Comment   : The noise model added a negative term after a wheel slip event. Shows on the motion tracker output. Fixed by clamping variance to a minimum of 1e-6.

E0591 | v5.9 | SMALL | Day 99 | Pi console (HDMI)
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Terminal  : Pi console (HDMI)

  accepted: 0/48 measurements
  pose drifts with no updates
Comment   : The Mahalanobis gate was too strict (chi2=3) for the noisy ToF. Shows as the filter never updating. Fixed by raising the gate to chi2=9 and testing with the outlier_test.

  filter diverges on the first turn
Comment   : Alpha was 10x too small, collapsing the sigma points. Shows as instant divergence on the first corner. Fixed by restoring alpha=1e-2 from the tuning run.

      K = P @ H.T @ inv(S)

E0601 | v6.0 | SMALL | Day 101 | ESP32 serial monitor

Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 motor_pid.py
  gain: 0.9 -> 2.4 (speed 1.00 m/s)
  steering step visible in the log
Comment   : The gain table stepped instead of interpolating at 1 m/s. Shows as a steering jerk on the test run. Fixed by linear interpolation between table rows.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0602 | v6.0 | SMALL | Day 101 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.0/pid_calibrate.py
Error     : Feedforward sign is opposite on reverse
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 pid_calibrate.py
  reverse + feedforward = wrong direction
  robot pulls to the left in reverse
Comment   : The feedforward steer term was not flipped when reversing. Shows only in the reverse logic tests. Fixed by multiplying by the direction sign.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0603 | v6.0 | SMALL | Day 101 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.0/motor_pid.py
Error     : Cubic spline overshoots past the goal
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 motor_pid.py
  end: x=2.01 y=0.03 (goal 2.00, 0.00)
  overshoot 1cm at the parking spot
Comment   : The spline endpoint constraint was dropped during smoothing. Shows on the spline_visualize plot. Fixed by re-imposing the fixed end condition.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0604 | v6.0 | SMALL | Day 101 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.0/pid_calibrate.py
Error     : Velocity profile outputs negative speeds
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 pid_calibrate.py
  v: -0.2 m/s at t=1.2s
  robot reverses mid-run
Comment   : The trapezoid profile went negative when the target was unreachable in time. Shows on the profile_test trace. Fixed by clamping v to zero and extending the profile.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0605 | v6.0 | SMALL | Day 101 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.0/motor_pid.py
Error     : Obstacle avoidance oscillates left-right
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 motor_pid.py
  steer: +12 -14 +11 -13 deg
  no progress toward the goal
Comment   : The avoidance gain fought the goal-pull twice per second. Shows as a stationary wobble in front of the obstacle. Fixed by adding a deadband and goal bias.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0606 | v6.0 | SMALL | Day 101 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.0/pid_calibrate.py
Error     : MPC solve fails on the first step
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 pid_calibrate.py
  cvxpy error: Problem status INFEASIBLE
  no control output
Comment   : The MPC constraints were infeasible because the horizon exceeded the track length. Shows every run at t=0. Fixed by shrinking the horizon and relaxing the terminal constraint.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0607 | v6.0 | SMALL | Day 101 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.0/motor_pid.py
Error     : PID output saturates then overcorrects
Terminal  : ESP32 serial monitor
  $ python3 motor_pid.py
  output: 0.98 -> -0.99 -> 0.97
  robot snakes down the straight
Comment   : The integral term wound up during the launch stall. Shows as oscillation every time the robot starts from standstill. Fixed by adding anti-windup to the integrator.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0608 | v6.0 | SMALL | Day 101 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------

  no steering command sent
Comment   : The path tangent was undefined at the first waypoint because the segment length was zero. Shows on the first lap tick. Fixed by skipping zero-length segments.

Terminal  : VS Code terminal
  $ python3 corner_test.py
  cross_track: nan  (first waypoint)
  no steering command sent

E0642 | v6.3 | SMALL | Day 107 | VS Code terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.3/feedforward_steer.py
Error     : Gain schedule jumps at the speed boundary
Terminal  : VS Code terminal
  $ python3 feedforward_steer.py
  gain: 0.9 -> 2.4 (speed 1.00 m/s)
  steering step visible in the log
Comment   : The gain table stepped instead of interpolating at 1 m/s. Shows as a steering jerk on the test run. Fixed by linear interpolation between table rows.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0643 | v6.3 | SMALL | Day 107 | VS Code terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.3/corner_test.py
Error     : Feedforward sign is opposite on reverse
Terminal  : VS Code terminal
  $ python3 corner_test.py
  reverse + feedforward = wrong direction
  robot pulls to the left in reverse
Comment   : The feedforward steer term was not flipped when reversing. Shows only in the reverse logic tests. Fixed by multiplying by the direction sign.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0644 | v6.3 | SMALL | Day 107 | VS Code terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.3/feedforward_steer.py
Error     : Cubic spline overshoots past the goal
Terminal  : VS Code terminal
  $ python3 feedforward_steer.py
  end: x=2.01 y=0.03 (goal 2.00, 0.00)
  overshoot 1cm at the parking spot
Comment   : The spline endpoint constraint was dropped during smoothing. Shows on the spline_visualize plot. Fixed by re-imposing the fixed end condition.

E0645 | v6.3 | BIG | Day 108 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.3/corner_test.py
Error     : Feedforward causes overshoot in sharp turns
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  ff: +0.8 -> overshoot 8 degrees
  corrective jerk after each corner
Comment   : The feedforward gain was tuned for gentle curves and overapplied on sharp ones. Shows as an overshoot after every hairpin. Fixed by scaling the feedforward with curvature.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0646 | v6.3 | BIG | Day 108 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.3/feedforward_steer.py
Error     : Anti-windup test fails with 30% overshoot
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  overshoot: 30% (target <10%)
  anti-windup not active
Comment   : The anti-windup flag was never set because the saturation check used the wrong sign. Shows on the windup_test. Fixed by comparing against the physical PWM limits.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0647 | v6.3 | BIG | Day 108 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.3/corner_test.py
Error     : Obstacle avoidance deadlocks in a U-shaped trap
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  avoid: stuck 12s at the same spot
  no escape direction found
Comment   : The local avoidance had no memory, so it oscillated inside the U-shape. Shows as the robot stuck at the trap on the avoidance_test. Fixed by adding a small heading memory.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0648 | v6.3 | BIG | Day 108 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  steer: +5 -5 +5 -5 deg at 5Hz
  overshoot visible in the video
Comment   : The servo PID gains were tuned on the bench, not on the moving robot. Shows as visible wobble at speed. Fixed by re-tuning with the servo_step_test on the floor.

  no progress toward the goal

Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 gain_schedule.py

  robot snakes down the straight
Comment   : The integral term wound up during the launch stall. Shows as oscillation every time the robot starts from standstill. Fixed by adding anti-windup to the integrator.

  overshoot 1cm at the parking spot
Comment   : The spline endpoint constraint was dropped during smoothing. Shows on the spline_visualize plot. Fixed by re-imposing the fixed end condition.

E0700 | v6.8 | SMALL | Day 117 | systemd journal

File      : history/v6.8/velocity_profile.py

Terminal  : systemd journal

  robot reverses mid-run

E0701 | v6.8 | SMALL | Day 117 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.8/profile_test.py
Error     : Obstacle avoidance oscillates left-right
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 profile_test.py
  steer: +12 -14 +11 -13 deg
  no progress toward the goal
Comment   : The avoidance gain fought the goal-pull twice per second. Shows as a stationary wobble in front of the obstacle. Fixed by adding a deadband and goal bias.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0702 | v6.8 | SMALL | Day 117 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.8/velocity_profile.py
Error     : MPC solve fails on the first step
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 velocity_profile.py
  cvxpy error: Problem status INFEASIBLE
  no control output
Comment   : The MPC constraints were infeasible because the horizon exceeded the track length. Shows every run at t=0. Fixed by shrinking the horizon and relaxing the terminal constraint.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0703 | v6.8 | SMALL | Day 117 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v6.8/profile_test.py
Error     : PID output saturates then overcorrects
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 profile_test.py
  output: 0.98 -> -0.99 -> 0.97
  robot snakes down the straight
Comment   : The integral term wound up during the launch stall. Shows as oscillation every time the robot starts from standstill. Fixed by adding anti-windup to the integrator.

Comment   : The lane score did not account for the current obstacle state. Shows on the race_strategy test. Fixed by adding the obstacle distance to the lane score.

E0841 | v8.0 | SMALL | Day 141 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steer_same.py
Error     : Task base double-schedules a callback
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 steer_same.py
  callback ran twice per tick
  motor command overwritten
Comment   : register_task was called twice for the same task in main. Shows as doubled control output. Fixed by guarding register_task with an id set.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0842 | v8.0 | SMALL | Day 141 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steering_common.py
Error     : Log severity mapping is wrong
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 steering_common.py
  ERROR logged as INFO
  failures hidden in the console
Comment   : The severity enum order differed between log_severity.py and the logger. Shows as mis-colored logs. Fixed by aligning the enum with the logger table.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0843 | v8.0 | SMALL | Day 141 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steer_same.py
Error     : YAML parse error in default_config.yaml
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 steer_same.py
  yaml.scanner.ScannerError: mapping values are not allowed here
  config failed to load
Comment   : A colon inside an unquoted string broke the YAML. Shows at startup, before the scheduler runs. Fixed by quoting the value.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0844 | v8.0 | SMALL | Day 141 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steering_common.py
Error     : Surprise rule key is unknown
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 steering_common.py
  KeyError: 'pillar_logic' in surprise_config
  rules ignored, defaults used
Comment   : The surprise_rules.yaml used a key the loader never read. Shows as silently running the default rule. Fixed by adding the key to the loader schema.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0845 | v8.0 | SMALL | Day 141 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steer_same.py
Error     : Steering mode switch race condition
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 steer_same.py
  mode: OPPOSITE (expected CRAB)
  one-frame wrong steering at the switch
Comment   : The mode switch flag was read while a write was in flight. Shows as a steering twitch at the switch point. Fixed by making the mode change atomic via the scheduler.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0846 | v8.0 | SMALL | Day 141 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steering_common.py
Error     : Heartbeat times out on a busy CPU
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 steering_common.py
  heartbeat lost: 1500ms
  motors cut mid-run
Comment   : The heartbeat thread starved when perception spiked. Shows as random motor cutouts. Fixed by moving the heartbeat to its own thread priority.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0847 | v8.0 | SMALL | Day 141 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steer_same.py
Error     : Health monitor false alarm on sensor 3
Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 steer_same.py
  sensor 3: FAIL (3 misses)
  actually fine, just slow
Comment   : The failure threshold of 3 misses was too tight for the ToF's slow warm-up. Shows as a red status at boot. Fixed by raising the threshold to 20 misses.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0848 | v8.0 | SMALL | Day 141 | systemd journal
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Terminal  : systemd journal
  $ python3 steering_common.py
  section: 5 (expected 4)
  strategy picks the wrong corner
Comment   : The section counter incremented on entry and exit of the line. Shows on the track_sections test. Fixed by incrementing only on entry.

E0849 | v8.0 | BIG | Day 142 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------

Error     : OOM killer kills the perception process
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  Out of memory: Killed process 1234 (vision)
  perception dead, robot runs blind
Comment   : The vision buffers leaked frames under load until the OOM killer acted. Shows in the system log as a killed process. Fixed by capping the frame queue length.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0850 | v8.0 | BIG | Day 142 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steering_common.py
Error     : Log severity floods the disk on a bad day
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  /var/log: 4.2GB of WARNING lines
  SD card 99% full
Comment   : A rate-limit bug let the error logger write at full speed. Shows as the SD card filling during a long test. Fixed by fixing the rate limiter and adding a log rotation.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0851 | v8.0 | BIG | Day 142 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steer_same.py
Error     : Scheduler deadlocks when a task raises
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  scheduler: task 'vision' raised, never resumed
  all other tasks stop
Comment   : An exception in one task killed the whole schedule because the loop never caught it. Shows as total system freeze mid-run. Fixed by wrapping each task in try/except and logging the failure.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0852 | v8.0 | BIG | Day 142 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.0/steering_common.py
Error     : Heartbeat failure cuts the motors at the start line
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  heartbeat: FAIL (start signal)
  motors disabled for 3s
Comment   : The heartbeat could not get CPU time during startup bursts, so the failsafe triggered at the worst moment. Shows as the robot refusing to move at the start. Fixed by boosting the heartbeat priority.

Comment   : The failure threshold of 3 misses was too tight for the ToF's slow warm-up. Shows as a red status at boot. Fixed by raising the threshold to 20 misses.

  strategy picks the wrong corner

Error     : Heartbeat failure cuts the motors at the start line

  heartbeat: FAIL (start signal)
  motors disabled for 3s
Comment   : The heartbeat could not get CPU time during startup bursts, so the failsafe triggered at the worst moment. Shows as the robot refusing to move at the start. Fixed by boosting the heartbeat priority.

  motors cut mid-run
Comment   : The heartbeat thread starved when perception spiked. Shows as random motor cutouts. Fixed by moving the heartbeat to its own thread priority.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0943 | v8.8 | SMALL | Day 157 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.8/health_monitor.py
Error     : Health monitor false alarm on sensor 3
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 health_monitor.py
  sensor 3: FAIL (3 misses)
  actually fine, just slow
Comment   : The failure threshold of 3 misses was too tight for the ToF's slow warm-up. Shows as a red status at boot. Fixed by raising the threshold to 20 misses.

E0944 | v8.8 | SMALL | Day 157 | Pi SSH terminal

Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  $ python3 heartbeat_protocol.py
  section: 5 (expected 4)
  strategy picks the wrong corner
Comment   : The section counter incremented on entry and exit of the line. Shows on the track_sections test. Fixed by incrementing only on entry.

E0945 | v8.8 | BIG | Day 158 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.8/health_monitor.py
Error     : OOM killer kills the perception process
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  Out of memory: Killed process 1234 (vision)
  perception dead, robot runs blind
Comment   : The vision buffers leaked frames under load until the OOM killer acted. Shows in the system log as a killed process. Fixed by capping the frame queue length.

E0946 | v8.8 | BIG | Day 158 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.8/heartbeat_protocol.py

Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  /var/log: 4.2GB of WARNING lines
  SD card 99% full
Comment   : A rate-limit bug let the error logger write at full speed. Shows as the SD card filling during a long test. Fixed by fixing the rate limiter and adding a log rotation.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0947 | v8.8 | BIG | Day 158 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.8/health_monitor.py
Error     : Scheduler deadlocks when a task raises
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  scheduler: task 'vision' raised, never resumed
  all other tasks stop
Comment   : An exception in one task killed the whole schedule because the loop never caught it. Shows as total system freeze mid-run. Fixed by wrapping each task in try/except and logging the failure.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
E0948 | v8.8 | BIG | Day 158 | Pi SSH terminal
----------------------------------------------------------------------
File      : history/v8.8/heartbeat_protocol.py
Error     : Heartbeat failure cuts the motors at the start line
Terminal  : Pi SSH terminal
  heartbeat: FAIL (start signal)
  motors disabled for 3s
Comment   : The heartbeat could not get CPU time during startup bursts, so the failsafe triggered at the worst moment. Shows as the robot refusing to move at the start. Fixed by boosting the heartbeat priority.

E1006 | v9.3 | BIG | Day 168 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------

  Core 0 panic'ed (StoreProhibited)
  store data at 0x3ffb0e10, pc 0x400df220
  Backtrace: 0x400df220:0x3ffb1f30 scheduler_tick
Comment   : The ESP32 scheduler wrote to a freed task context after the task was deleted. Shows as a core dump mid-run. Fixed by deferring task deletion to the idle hook.

Error     : Full pipeline crashes 2 seconds after the start

      scheduler.start()
    File "pi/system/scheduler.py", line 31
      await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
  TypeError: 'Task' object is not callable
Comment   : The scheduler shadowed asyncio.Task with its own class. Shows as a crash right after boot, before the first motor command. Fixed by renaming the internal class to ScheduledTask.

E1067 | v9.8 | BIG | Day 178 | ESP32 serial monitor
----------------------------------------------------------------------

  E (89430) task_wdt: Task watchdog got triggered

  Rebooting... (loop)
Comment   : The motor task stalled on a blocked UART write during the final run. Shows as a reboot loop exactly at high load. Fixed by setting the UART write to non-blocking with a timeout.

  autostart failed, manual run works
Comment   : The systemd service ran as the wrong user without dialout rights. Shows only on autostart, never on manual runs. Fixed by adding the service user to the dialout group.

Comment   : The ESP32 scheduler wrote to a freed task context after the task was deleted. Shows as a core dump mid-run. Fixed by deferring task deletion to the idle hook.
